# Every ordered pair of Line_Break classes, as two codepoints,
# and whether a line break is allowed between them under each of
# CSS Text's line-break values, in a neutral writing system and
# then in Japanese. Six columns rather than three because four of
# the tailorings apply only to Chinese and Japanese text, and
# under a neutral system normal and strict are the same rules.
# `anywhere` is not here: it does not read Line_Break at all.
# A regression record: the oracle is LineBreakTest.txt and the
# ICU differential. Regenerate with GUNI_BREAK_DUMP=pairs.
# left right strict normal loose strict_ja normal_ja loose_ja
